                      break'''

      print("[OK] Lógica de início dos dados corrigida!")
  else:
      print("[AVISO] Trecho não encontrado. Tentando substituição mais flexível...")
      import re
      padrao = r'inicio_dados = 0\s*\n\s*for i, linha in enumerate\(linhas\):\s*\n\s*if "Centro de Custo x Conta" in linha:\s*\n\s*inicio_dados = i \+ 1\s*\n\s*break\s*\n\s*\n\s*if inicio_dados < len\(linhas\) and "COFEN/BR" in linhas\[inicio_dados\]:\s*\n\s*inicio_dados \+= 1'
      if re.search(padrao, c, re.DOTALL):

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

          if v is None: return "0,00"

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"

          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      pdf = FPDF(orientation="P", unit="mm", format="A4")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"

          if v is None: return "0,00"

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def pct(va, vb):
          if not vb or vb == 0: return "0,00%"
          return f"{(((va - vb) / abs(vb)) * 100):,.2f}%".replace(".", ",")

      def fmt(v):
          if v is None: return "0,00"
          s = f"{v:,.2f}"
          # Formato: 1,234,567.89 -> 1.234.567,89
          return s.replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")
